# References from the 2022 AHA/ACC/HFSA heart failure guideline



Source: Heidenreich PA, Bozkurt B, Aguilar D, et al. *2022 AHA/ACC/HFSA Guideline for the Management of Heart Failure.* J Am Coll Cardiol. 2022;79:e263-e421. DOI: 10.1016/j.jacc.2021.12.012.



Reference pages e371-e410 are transcribed below in source order. The guideline numbers references within sections rather than as one continuous bibliography, so section-local numbering is preserved. Page headings are retained to make cross-checking against the source straightforward.



Evidence-table citations in the 807-page supplemental materials were used for SEA reconciliation but are not duplicated here as a second bibliography.
